# Owner combined W1 walkthrough verdict (2026-10-17)

Owner (verbatim): "I have completed the combined walkthrough. I am still seeing the UI is not still faithfull to the mockup. For example, the site  overview page is different from the one in the mockup.In the foundation screen, the 'view configuration radiness' panel takes a lot of space and is styled inferior to the mockup, for this screen the mockup makes configured topology and identity and operation obligations immediately prominent while the implemented version has "What is configured for this period?" panel. The mockup looks much more user friendly. The implemented  version has positives such as policy in the foundation screen but it looks as if that needs to get a better home or presented better.This same these extends across all the screens, mockup screen does a better job and there is implementation drift, i don't want that to be the case going forward as well and i do't want to comment on the same things mutliple times."

Disposition: W1 NOT accepted. Root cause (coordinator): the W1-UX pass layered plain-language answer cards above existing layouts instead of rebuilding each screen to the v69 panel structure. Remedy: a mockup-as-layout-spec contract enforced by an automated panel-structure test per screen (v69 `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json` titles and order) plus side-by-side screenshot review against the mock before any owner session; then a W1 structural fidelity rework; the same contract applies to all W2+ UI slices. W1 closes only after the owner accepts the reworked screens.

# W1 combined owner walkthrough

Final version, written at W1-UX-06 phase 2 (2026-10-17). It covers all 26 W1 Site screens plus the S4-08 fuel-price history. Follow it top to bottom. Each part prepares one test case, serves it, shows you what to look at, and then cleans it up again.

## What changed in W1, in plain words

W1 did not add new features or new data. It changed how the existing Site screens explain themselves, so that each one reads like the v69 mock-up while saying no more than the stored records support.

- **Most screens now open with an answer card.** (Exceptions: some held or empty states draw no picture, listed in owner note 10, and a few refusal or not-yet-stored states show no card at all, marked "not run" in their part's capture table.) At the top is the question the screen answers, for example "Which site should I open?" or "What can we rely on?". Below it come a short plain answer, a state line, a small picture (bars, a step strip or a time strip), "What this means" and "What to do", and a link to the place that holds the detail.
- **The state line is always visible.** It gives the state, what the answer covers, and "As of <time>". When the record has no time, it says "As of: time not recorded", and never pretends the time is now.
- **Codes and IDs are tucked away.** References, versions and codes sit in one closed "How this was worked out" section at the bottom of each card. The main reading uses plain words.
- **Unknown is shown as unknown.** A missing value is drawn as a hatched "not known" mark, never as a zero bar. Refused, unavailable, stale or withdrawn reads say so in words.
- **A current amount is shown only when the current read proves it is current and priced.** Otherwise the screen says "No current amount can be shown" or "No price recorded", and never shows zero. Earlier recorded amounts appear only on history pages, each labelled as history.
- **Set-up and health stay separate.** The Readiness badge now says "Set up to report", which means configuration only. Gateway contact is shown on Health, and contact never stands in for equipment condition.
- **Test data is labelled.** All data in this walkthrough is authored, generated or simulated test data, not field readings. Do not compare numbers between different cases; each case is its own story.

## Before you start (once)

1. Open PowerShell and go to the folder you will run everything from, then confirm it is on the merged revision:
   ```powershell
   cd C:\Users\assef\Bineyame\AssetOpsV1
   git log --oneline -1
   ```
   The line shown must be the commit the coordinator gives you (the W1 closeout merge). If it is not, stop and ask. Use only this folder for the whole walkthrough.
2. Docker Desktop is running, with the AssetOps Postgres container on port 55432.
3. In **every** PowerShell window you open for this walkthrough, paste these lines first (they move to the walkthrough folder and set the paths every command below uses):
   ```powershell
   cd C:\Users\assef\Bineyame\AssetOpsV1
   $wt = (Get-Location).Path
   $env:PYTHONPATH = "$wt;$wt\backend;$wt\contracts;$wt\gateway;$wt\simulator"
   ```
   The Python used is `$wt\.venv\Scripts\python.exe` (the `.venv\Scripts\python.exe` lines below, run from `$wt`).
4. Site files and dependencies (once). Dependencies changed during W1, so install them; the site files are copied only if missing:
   ```powershell
   New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force "$wt\var\sites" | Out-Null
   if (-not (Test-Path "$wt\var\sites\kobo-001.yaml")) { Write-Host "kobo-001.yaml is missing in var\sites; ask the coordinator for the site files" }
   cd $wt\frontend
   npm.cmd ci
   cd $wt
   ```
   Stop any AssetOps dev server already running from this folder before `npm.cmd ci`.
5. Each part uses **two windows**. Window 1 runs the test-case tool (its API server). Window 2 runs the screens:
   ```powershell
   cd $wt\frontend
   $env:ASSETOPS_API_PORT = "<API port named in the part>"
   npm.cmd run dev -- --port <web port named in the part> --strictPort
   ```
   Open the addresses on `http://localhost:<web port>`. Use `localhost`, not `127.0.0.1`.
6. Open the mock-up beside the app at `http://localhost:<web port>/mockups/v69/index.html#/<screen id>`.
7. **On every screen, check:**
   - the answer card reads in plain words;
   - the state line (state, scope, "As of ...") is visible while "How this was worked out" is closed;
   - pressing Tab from the top eventually reaches "How this was worked out";
   - copying the address into a new tab opens the same view;
   - reloading keeps the same view.
   Narrow the browser to phone width (about 390 px) and check that nothing scrolls sideways.
8. **Run one part at a time.** When a part is finished, stop both windows with Ctrl+C and run its clean-up lines before you start the next part.

## Part 1. Sites, Foundation, Readiness, single line diagram (`v01a_foundation_walkthrough.py`)

Screens: sites, foundation, readiness, sld. Run log: `.agent/W1-UX-06-evidence/v01a-*.txt`.

### 1. Prerequisites (one time)
- Docker Postgres running on 127.0.0.1:55432.
- `var/sites/kobo-001.yaml` present in this folder (see "Before you start", step 4) (`var/` is ignored by git).
- The shared test database `assetops_s4cases` must exist. If `docker exec assetopsv1-postgres-1 psql -U assetops -lqt` does not list it, run once:
```
.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools\s4_finding_cases.py run
```

### 2. Commands (PowerShell, in order)
```
cd $wt
$env:PYTHONPATH="$wt;$wt\backend;$wt\contracts;$wt\gateway;$wt\simulator"
$T="--db","assetops_v01awalk_w1ux06","--empty-db","assetops_v01aempty_w1ux06","--work-name","v01a-w1ux06"
.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools\v01a_foundation_walkthrough.py @T prepare
.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools\v01a_foundation_walkthrough.py @T links --web-port 5460 --empty-web-port 5461
.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools\v01a_foundation_walkthrough.py @T serve --port 8960 --web-port 5460 --empty-port 8961 --empty-web-port 5461
```
Second window (stocked site):
```
cd $wt\frontend; $env:ASSETOPS_API_PORT="8960"; npm.cmd run dev -- --port 5460 --strictPort
```
Third window (no site stored):
```
cd $wt\frontend; $env:ASSETOPS_API_PORT="8961"; npm.cmd run dev -- --port 5461 --strictPort
```

### 3. Ports and addresses
APIs 8960 (stocked) and 8961 (empty); web 5460 and 5461. Mock: `http://localhost:5460/mockups/v69/index.html#/<id>`.

| Screen | Address | Mock |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Sites | http://localhost:5460/sites | `#/sites` |
| Sites, none stored | http://localhost:5461/sites | `#/sites` |
| Site model, in effect now | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation | `#/foundation` |
| Site model, scheduled | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation?at=2026-10-22T00:00:00Z | `#/foundation` |
| Site model, before any stored version | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation?at=2026-09-01T00:00:00Z | `#/foundation` |
| Readiness, now | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation/readiness | `#/readiness` |
| Readiness, imported period | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation/readiness?at=2026-09-30T00:00:00Z | `#/readiness` |
| Diagram, now | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation/sld | `#/sld` |
| Diagram, battery chosen | http://localhost:5460/sites/KOBO-001/foundation/sld?at=2026-10-22T00:00:00Z&component=battery | `#/sld` |

### 4. What to look for
- The bold first sentence of the top card answers the question in plain words (for example "9 pieces of equipment and 4 of 5 operating settings are recorded").
- Under it, with "How this was worked out" still closed, the state box shows the state, the site and version, and an "As of" time. On Sites it says "As of: time not recorded" (the list has no read time).
- The small picture (bars or setting tiles) is beside the answer; Sites has none by design.
- The link under the card goes to the next place (Readiness, Single line diagram, Health).
- Scheduled view: a yellow line says this is not the configuration in effect now.
- Before any stored version: only the state line shows, no card (by design). Look by eye only.
- Battery chosen: the battery is highlighted and stays chosen after a reload.
- Keyboard: press Tab from the top of the page; it reaches "How this was worked out".
- Exact link: paste the address in a new tab; the same view opens. Reload: nothing changes.

### 5. Data label
Authored test configuration of KOBO-001 (imported and published Foundation versions). Not field readings.

### 6. Stop and clean up
Ctrl+C in all three windows, then:
```
.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools\v01a_foundation_walkthrough.py @T drop
```
`drop` removes both databases and the work folder `%LOCALAPPDATA%\AssetOps\v01a-w1ux06`.

### 3. Ports and addresses

### 4. What to look for
- P01: the bold answer says "No operating objective is stated for this day"; the green box "Using the latest checked readings" shows the day, site and "As of" time while "How this was worked out" is closed.
- The fuel-cost card shows "0.00 to 15.82 USD" with a two-bar range and says it is prepared example data.
- The link "Open this day in Performance > Dispatch" goes to the day's dispatch view.
- Not assessed: the answer says it cannot judge this yet, with no amount.
- If more than 5 minutes passed since `tick`, the page says the schedule stopped and hides the amount; run `tick` again.
- Keyboard: Tab reaches "How this was worked out". Exact link: paste in a new tab, same day. Reload: same day.

### 5. Data label
Authored demonstration cases (P01, P02) and generated simulated readings (ordinary day). Not field readings.

### 6. Stop and clean up
Ctrl+C in both windows, then:
```
.venv\Scripts\python.exe tools\s5_overview_walkthrough.py @T drop
```

### 4. What to look for

- F02: "This result may miss newer readings" (a newer record is waiting to be checked); the recorded version stays visible.
- History: "Earlier version, kept for the record", with the version timeline and links "Select the latest version", "Open version 1 of the Finding" and "Open version 1's evidence".
- Keyboard: Tab reaches "How this was worked out". Exact link: paste in a new tab, same Finding and version. Reload: same page.

`prepare` takes about 3.5 minutes and must end with "0 failed checks". The recorded moments T1/T2/T3 are new on every prepare, so always copy addresses from `links`. `serve` keeps running until Ctrl+C.

- T1 / T3: "Prices as recorded at an earlier moment"; the prices and the history are for the same moment; the address keeps `recorded_at` after reload.
- Closed source: every version is listed without an amount, and the page says the source is closed. Mixed source: every recorded amount is withheld ("Sources do not agree").

## Part 17. The left menu (W1-UX-07)

No extra setup: look at the menu on any page you already have open in an earlier part (for example Sites in Part 1), and open the mock-up beside it at `#/sites`.

What to look for:
- The menu now shows the whole mock-up menu: six groups, OPERATIONS, INTELLIGENCE, WORK, ASSURANCE, BUSINESS and ADMIN, with eleven entries in the mock-up's order.
- Only **Sites** and **Findings** open a page. The other nine (Portfolio, Assets, Actions, Incidents, Maintenance, Operational records, Evidence, Financials, Settings) are greyed out and each says "Not available yet" under its name. Clicking them does nothing, and pressing Tab skips them.
- "Not available yet" here means the product has no all-sites page for that entry yet. It does not mean a site has no data: a site's own Assets, Work, Financials and Evidence tabs still work from the site.
- Under the menu, a WORKSPACE section holds "Open Simulator Lab" (shown because the Simulator Lab is switched on in this build). There is no longer a separate bar above the page for it.
- The mock-up's "Demo workspace / 3 sites" line is left out: that count is example data.
- At phone width (about 390 px) the menu sits above the page and wraps; every entry keeps its "Not available yet" with it, and nothing scrolls sideways.

Captured (code at W1-UX-07): `node tools/w1-ux-07-rail-evidence.mjs` 118/118 with the Lab on and 62/62 with the Lab off, at 1440 and 390 px, beside the mock rail.

## Owner's notes: open questions to decide during or after the walk

These are not defects in W1. They are choices the reviews left for you. Mark each one accept, change, or later.

1. **Money words differ between Overview and Financials (W1-UX-02 L4).** The same money state reads differently on the two screens. For example, the Overview says "Fuel quantity not known" and "Fuel cost out of date", while Financials says "Not calculated: no fuel amount, so no cost" and "No current amount can be shown". The Overview's "Upstream out of date" label is jargon. Should the two screens use one set of words?
2. **Financials "no current amount" states show "As of: time not recorded" (W1-UX-02 L6).** The record does carry the time the earlier cost was recorded, but that time dates the old cost, not today's "no current amount" judgement. Should that time be shown, labelled as "earlier cost recorded at ..."? This would need an Architect decision on a read time for the money view.
3. **The Readiness strip and gateway contact.** The strip is now titled "Set-up steps" and covers configuration only. Gateway contact, including stale contact, is shown separately on Health and in the Gateway status panel. Is that split right, or should the strip also show a contact line from the health read? That would be a new read for that screen.
4. **New cards sit above the older panels, so some facts appear twice.** On the Action, Plan, Evidence and Verification screens, the outcome, failed limits and source appear first in the card and again in the older panel below it (W1-UX-04 review item 8). The Dispatch block strip and the per-period block chooser also both select a block (W1-UX-03). Folding these together is a later slice with its own test changes. Is the current density acceptable for now?
5. **Long pages.** Overview and Financials keep their older sections below the new answer card, so they are much longer than the mock-up.
6. **Mock-up content with no record behind it is not built.** On these screens it is either left out or shown as "not available yet":
   - directory counts and type chips;
   - the five-objective strip;
   - Finding priority and confidence chips;
   - the revenue, asset and realised-savings tiles on Financials;
   - Scenarios;
   - editable Assumptions with an impact preview.
7. **Evidence "As of" uses the latest delivery received.** The Evidence read has no read time of its own, so its "As of" can only understate how current it is.
8. **Action Overview names no data source.** Its summary record carries no source label, so the card says so and points to Verification for it.
9. **Dispatch Analysis money sentence.** It still waits for G-04 to tag the fuel/money sentence as missing. Until then a test allowance remains.
10. **Some held or empty states draw no small picture.** The final capture recorded a failed "the card has a small visual" check for these states, which have nothing to draw:
    - the latest and the unavailable Recommendation (both held);
    - the withdrawn trial's held Recommendation;
    - Site Work with nothing to do (proved zero);
    - Assumptions with no price recorded.
    Is a card without a picture right for these, or should they show a "not known" or "nothing scheduled" mark?
11. **The single-line diagram at phone width.** At 390 px the diagram is wider than the screen, so it scrolls sideways inside its own box. The page itself does not scroll sideways.
12. **A Recommendation whose read is unavailable is labelled as held.** In the Action case it reads "Held: an earlier trial is still open". Check that this is the wording you want for that case.
13. **Dispatch Analysis addresses.** The Dispatch tool prints only the day address, so open Analysis from the page's own link rather than typing an address.
14. **Menu height and status text size at phone width.** At 390 px the full menu sits above the page and is about 575 px tall, and the "Not available yet" status text is small (9 px, readable contrast). Judge whether the menu should collapse on phones or the status text be larger.

## Gate

W1 closes only after you have walked this script and recorded your acceptance. Screenshots and passing checks do not count as acceptance, and nothing in this script promotes a screen or panel.

codex
The owner’s verdict is clear: W1 is not accepted because the current screens drift from the mockup’s layout. I’m mapping the 26 W1 screens against the v69 panel inventory and current components so the rework plan identifies where each piece of content belongs.

/*
 * Design tokens: the one set of type and colour values every surface uses.
 *
 * Read from `Docs/UI Design/Motivation/ScreenMockups.png` rather than invented:
 * a dark navy rail with a bright blue active item, white cards on a pale
 * blue-grey page, pill badges, and a single blue accent for links and the one
 * primary action. The mockups are authoritative about information architecture
 * and visual vocabulary, never about capability inventory or navigation, so
 * what is borrowed here is the look and nothing else.
 *
 * Contrast: every foreground/background pair below meets WCAG AA for its size
 * (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and non-text boundaries). The badge
 * tones are checked against their own backgrounds, not against the page.
 *
 * Colour never carries meaning on its own anywhere in this system. A badge
 * always renders the word it means, and the tones exist to keep the four
 * vocabularies visually distinct, not to replace their text.
 */

  --font-sans:

# W1 UX pass - Architect answers (2026-10-16)

Decision: `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`. Codex Architect, under `.ai/ROLE_CONFIG.md`. This answers the two questions in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` and governs W1-UX-01 through W1-UX-05. It amends the proposed disclosure-only pattern and approves a bounded vocabulary. Owner visual acceptance remains the combined W1 walkthrough.

## 1. Deeper analysis placement - replace the disclosure-only recommendation

The owner's T028 direction and `D-2026-10-04-plain-language-analysis-views` require a separate Analysis destination for substantive method, coverage, comparisons and calculation. Retain the existing Finding, Dispatch and Site Evidence `Overview | Analysis` pattern and the v69 contextual tab hierarchy. For other W1 detail roots, use an existing meaningful Analysis, Evidence, Plan, History, Verification or equivalent detail tab where it owns the deeper material. If a root has substantive technical explanation but no suitable destination, add one contextual **Analysis** tab in its owning W1 slice; do not add an empty tab or duplicate a record trail already owned by Evidence or History. The W1-UX-01 shared component may provide a `View analysis` link with selected Site, period, revision and object context; it must not make a closed disclosure the only route to substantive explanation.

Directory rows, index rows and compact Overview cards do not each need a tab strip. They link to the owning object's detail or Analysis destination. A closed inline **How this was worked out** disclosure is allowed for a few exact references, IDs, versions, raw reasons or support fields on a detail page. It is supplementary; a long table, causal explanation or evidence trail belongs in its contextual destination. Keep exact record links and historical selectors usable. Do not use a separate global expert mode.

At 1440 px, keep the contextual tabs visible under the page heading and link cards to the selected Analysis view. At 390 px, keep the **same named destination** as a keyboard-accessible tab or view link in the normal page flow, with wrapping or horizontal scrolling and an obvious active state; never replace it with a disclosure because the viewport is narrow. A small exact-reference disclosure may remain closed at either width. Material missing evidence, stale/pending/refused state, scope, and consequence limits stay beside the headline at both widths, outside any tab or disclosure. Native disclosure semantics and readable graphic text apply where a small disclosure is used.

## 2. Product vocabulary - accept with tighter boundaries

**Site, Evidence, Finding, Action and Verification** are approved domain labels for navigation and object identity. Explain their meaning in nearby ordinary language on first use in a view: a Finding is a bounded conclusion about an assessed period; an Action is proposed or recorded work; Evidence is the records used; Verification is a measured trial outcome when a result exists. **Analysis** is an approved destination label. Keep canonical route/tab names, including Verification, while a result card can say **Trial outcome**. A label grants no positive claim: `Finding` does not mean confirmed fault, `Action` does not mean applied, and `Verification` does not mean verified. Prefer **What the readings can support** over `claim limit`, **Reporting status** for source contact, and **Battery condition: cannot yet be judged** until a reviewed physical-condition read exists.

Default prose, headings, chart labels, badges, collapsed summaries and accessible names must not expose raw enums or reason codes (`CURRENT`, `UNASSESSED`, `VERIFIED`, etc.), UUIDs, digests, rule/model versions, cursor/watermark mechanics, internal analytics terms (`claim ceiling`, `claim limit`, `basis fingerprint`, `typed reason`) or simulator/development vocabulary (`run`, `binding`, `stage`, `commit`, `fixture`, `payload`) in its implementation sense. Ordinary operational language such as **the generator ran** is fine. Do not render a raw code in the collapsed control's accessible label. Source-owned or user-owned exact names may appear where identity itself is the subject, with a label and explanation; exact IDs and technical codes remain available in labelled fields in Analysis/Evidence or the small detail disclosure. No whole-screen or whole-response vocabulary exemption.

The ban on implementation language also applies inside Analysis and Evidence: technical depth explains the site's operation, evidence and limits, not AssetOps internals. Raw exact references are allowed there as labelled data for traceability. Authored example or generated test data must retain its explicit source qualification; it must not be presented as field equipment readings. W1-UX-01 should apply these rules to rendered API text, graphics, accessibility text and the proposed vocabulary scan.

## Claim order and implementation guard

**Honest state outranks presentation fidelity.** The selected, authorized read owns currentness, zero, amount, source, coverage, outcome and historical status. A headline, graphic or shorter label must not claim more than that read proves. Keep unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, unassessed and proved-zero distinct. A fresh receipt is not fresh source facts or current analysis; source contact is not equipment condition; an applied Action is not a verified result; a modelled fuel-cost exposure is not realized loss or savings. Do not copy v69 illustrative numbers into product data. Put material qualifiers next to the answer even when the detailed basis has its own tab.

This is presentation direction only. W1 slices retain their source contracts, exact links, review gates and combined owner walkthrough. No backend or task-packet change is authorized by this decision.

/**
 * S4-03: the named child views of the canonical Finding root. Each is
 * `/sites/{site}/findings/{finding}/{view}[?revision_id=]` and keeps the same
 * exact-version parameter. The frame's switch (`SiteFindingFrame`) renders
 * each view from this list (Evidence: phase 2, History: phase 3).
 * S6-01b adds Recommendation (`recommendation:1`), which keeps the same
 * `?revision_id=` so the recommendation is always for an exact version.
 */
export const FINDING_CHILD_VIEWS = ["evidence", "history", "recommendation"] as const;
export type FindingChildView = (typeof FINDING_CHILD_VIEWS)[number];
export const SITE_FINDING_VIEW_ROUTE_PATH = `${SITE_FINDING_ROUTE_PATH}/:findingView`;

export function siteFindingViewHref(
  siteId: string,
  findingId: string,
  view: FindingChildView,
  revisionId?: string | null,
): string {
  const base = `${siteFindingsHref(siteId)}/${encodeURIComponent(findingId)}/${view}`;
  return revisionId ? `${base}?revision_id=${encodeURIComponent(revisionId)}` : base;
}

/**
 * The mini-grid Site Overview body (T027 phase 2, AC11, AC22).
 *
 * "Is this site meeting its operating objective, and what matters now?" in the
 * v6.9 8.18 frame: the objective strip, one card per profile domain, the
 * selected-window timeline, then Findings. Everything shown is the backend
 * Overview read model's, computed from accepted evidence only; the profile
 * decides which cards render and in which order, never their state.
 *
 * No analytic exists yet, so every card and the objective strip show the
 * neutral "No assessment yet". "Unknown" is never used for that. A metric whose
 * signal has no record a metric can use in the window (none, none GOOD, no
 * interval span, or only overlapping records) shows no value and names the gap
 * with the backend's reason; the card lists the gap from the backend's
 * coverage, not from the profile. The timeline is context, not a status source.
 *
 * Evidence links open the Site > Evidence record inspector at the address
 * `evidenceHref` builds (`/sites/{id}/evidence?signal=DEV/SIG&record=MSG`);
 * the shell supplies the builder, because the substrate does not know routes.
 */

import { AwaitingEvidenceMigration } from "./AwaitingEvidenceMigration";
import { awaitingMigration } from "./siteEvidenceClient";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { SiteWindowOverview, type WindowOverviewLinks } from "./SiteWindowOverview";
import type { FindingIndexClient } from "./findingIndexClient";

import { Badge, Fact, FactList, Panel } from "../ui";
import { AssessedPeriodTimeline, FuelRange } from "./assessmentGraphics";
import {
  capabilityPresentation,
  orderedOverviewCards,
  presentationProfileFor,
  type DomainPresentationProfile,
} from "./presentationProfile";
import type { EvidenceRead } from "./siteEvidenceClient";
import type {
  OverviewCard,
  OverviewInstantPoint,
  OverviewIntervalPoint,
  OverviewMetric,
  OverviewSeries,
  OverviewSelection,
  SiteOverviewClient,
  SiteOverviewReadModel,
  SiteWindowSummary,
} from "./siteOverviewClient";
import { isSiteWindowSummary } from "./siteOverviewClient";

export const SITE_OVERVIEW_LOADING = "Loading the site's overview.";

export interface SiteOverviewProps {
  siteId: string;
  client: SiteOverviewClient;
  /** The inspector address for one accepted record of one signal. */
  evidenceHref: (signal: string, messageId: string) => string;
  /** The Site > Evidence destination. */
  evidencePageHref: string;
  /** T028: the Finding detail address for one assessment id. */
  findingHref?: (assessmentId: string) => string;
  /** T028: Performance > Dispatch, optionally selecting one interval. */
  dispatchHref?: (assessmentId?: string) => string;
  /**
   * The Analysis of an assessed period: the Finding's Analysis tab, or
   * Dispatch's Analysis view for a Needed period without a Finding.
   */
  analysisHref?: (assessmentId: string, isFinding: boolean) => string;
  /** S5-01: destinations for an imported Site's selected-window Overview. */
  windowLinks?: WindowOverviewLinks;
  /** S5-02: the Site Finding index read for the selected day's list, handed in by the shell (imported Sites only). */
  findingIndex?: FindingIndexClient;
}

/** S5-01: the address parameters an imported Site's Overview keeps across reloads. */
const SELECTION_KEYS = ["date", "finding_id", "finding_revision_id"] as const;

function selectionOf(params: URLSearchParams): OverviewSelection {
  return {
    date: params.get("date"),
    finding_id: params.get("finding_id"),
    finding_revision_id: params.get("finding_revision_id"),
  };
}

function selectionKey(selection: OverviewSelection): string {
  return SELECTION_KEYS.map((k) => selection[k] ?? "").join("|");
}

/** What the served day and Finding would read as in the address (the server's values, copied). */
function servedSelection(summary: SiteWindowSummary): OverviewSelection {
  return {
    date: summary.window.utc_date,
    finding_id: summary.known_finding?.id ?? null,
    finding_revision_id: summary.known_finding?.revision_id ?? null,
  };
}

export function SiteOverview({
  siteId,
  client,
  evidenceHref,
  evidencePageHref,
  findingHref,
  dispatchHref,
  analysisHref,
  windowLinks,
  findingIndex,
}: SiteOverviewProps) {
  const [result, setResult] =
    useState<EvidenceRead<SiteOverviewReadModel | SiteWindowSummary> | null>(null);
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const selection = selectionOf(params);
  const key = selectionKey(selection);
  // The selection the shown result already answers: writing the served day and Finding into the address must
  // not read the same thing again.
  const answered = useRef<string | null>(null);
  // S5-02: the Finding list's reload reads the Overview again even when the address is unchanged.
  const [reloads, setReloads] = useState(0);
  const reload = () => {
    answered.current = null;
    setReloads((n) => n + 1);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (answered.current === `${siteId}#${key}`) {
      return;
    }
    let current = true;
    setResult(null);
    client.getOverview(siteId, {}, selection).then((read) => {
      if (!current) {
        return;
      }
      setResult(read);
      if (read.status === "loaded" && isSiteWindowSummary(read.value)) {
        // Date change and reload keep the served day and the selected Finding version (packet "Outcome").
        const served = servedSelection(read.value);
        const servedKey = selectionKey(served);
        answered.current = `${siteId}#${servedKey}`;
        if (servedKey !== key) {
          const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
          for (const k of SELECTION_KEYS) {
            const v = served[k];
            if (v) {
              next.set(k, v);
            } else {
              next.delete(k);
            }
          }
          setParams(next, { replace: true });
        }
      } else {
        answered.current = null;
      }
    });
    return () => {
      current = false;
    };
    // `params` is read through `key`, which names every parameter this read uses.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [client, siteId, key, reloads]);

  const selectDay = (day: string) => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
    next.set("date", day);
    next.delete("finding_id");
    next.delete("finding_revision_id");
    setParams(next);
  };

  if (result === null) {
    return <p>{SITE_OVERVIEW_LOADING}</p>;
  }
  if (awaitingMigration(result)) {
    return <AwaitingEvidenceMigration surface="overview" />;
  }
  if (result.status !== "loaded") {
    return (
      <Panel
        heading="Overview unavailable"
        headingId="site-overview-unavailable-heading"
      >
        <p>
          The Overview could not be read
          {result.status === "unavailable" && result.message !== null
            ? `: ${result.message}`
            : "."}{" "}
          This is a statement about the read, not about the site&apos;s
          evidence.
        </p>
      </Panel>
    );
  }

  if (isSiteWindowSummary(result.value)) {
    if (windowLinks === undefined) {
      return (
        <Panel heading="Overview unavailable" headingId="site-overview-unavailable-heading">
          <p>This screen has no destinations for the selected-day Overview.</p>
        </Panel>
      );
    }
    return (
      <SiteWindowOverview
        summary={result.value}
        links={windowLinks}
        onSelectDay={selectDay}
        findingIndex={findingIndex}
        onReload={reload}
      />
    );
  }
  const overview = result.value;
  const profile = presentationProfileFor(overview.site_type);
  const cards = orderedOverviewCards(profile, overview.cards);
  const link = (signal: string, messageId: string) =>
    evidenceHref(signal, messageId);
  // The Overview links to the backend's Findings; it never recomputes one.
  const findings = overview.findings as OverviewFinding[];
  const findingIds = new Set(
    findings.flatMap((f) => (f.finding_id === null ? [] : [f.finding_id])),
  );

  return (
    <div
      className="site-overview"
      data-overview-state={
        overview.window.start === null ? "empty" : "populated"
      }
    >
      <ObjectiveStrip
        overview={overview}
        evidencePageHref={evidencePageHref}
        link={link}
      />
      {cards.length === 0 ? null : (
        <section
          className="overview-cards"
          aria-labelledby="site-overview-domains-heading"
        >
          <h2
            className="overview-cards__heading"
            id="site-overview-domains-heading"
          >
            Domains
          </h2>
          <div className="overview-cards__grid">
            {cards.map(({ label, card }) => (
              <DomainCard
                key={card.domain_id}
                label={label}
                card={card}
                profile={profile}
                link={link}
                findingIds={findingIds}
                findingHref={findingHref}
                dispatchHref={dispatchHref}
                analysisHref={analysisHref}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="overview-note">{overview.interval_statement}</p>
        </section>
      )}
      <Timeline overview={overview} />
      <Panel heading="Findings" headingId="site-overview-findings-heading">
        <p>{overview.findings_statement}</p>
        {findings.length === 0 || findingHref === undefined ? null : (
          <ul className="cell-list" data-overview-findings>
            {findings.map((finding) => (
              <li key={finding.assessment_id}>
                <Link
                  to={findingHref(finding.assessment_id)}
                  data-overview-finding-link={finding.assessment_id}
                >
                  {finding.reading === undefined
                    ? "Assessed generator period"
                    : `${finding.reading.outcome}: ${finding.reading.headline}`}
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Panel>
    </div>
  );
}

type LinkBuilder = (signal: string, messageId: string) => string;

/** The Overview's `findings`: the backend's lineage summaries, as `/findings` lists them. */
interface OverviewFinding {
  assessment_id: string;
  finding_id: string | null;
  label: string;
  summary: string;
  /** The backend's plain reading (`plain.reading`), read time. */
  reading?: { outcome: string; headline: string };
}

function ObjectiveStrip({
  overview,
  evidencePageHref,
  link,
}: {
  overview: SiteOverviewReadModel;
  evidencePageHref: string;
  link: LinkBuilder;
}) {
  const { window, objective } = overview;
  const empty = window.start === null;
  return (
    <Panel
      heading="Operating objective"
      headingId="site-overview-objective-heading"
    >
      <FactList>
        <Fact term="Site assessment">
          <Badge tone="neutral">{objective.assessment.label}</Badge>{" "}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";

            const outcome = await foundation.publishBindings(siteId, {
              valid_from: validFrom,
              bindings: bindings.map(bindingPublicationItem),
              note,
            });
            if (outcome.status === "published") setRefresh((count) => count + 1);
            return outcome;
          }}

              asset_code: assetCode,
              value,
              valid_from: validFrom,
            });

+# W1 structural fidelity: Architect contract and rework plan (2026-10-17)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. The owner rejected the combined W1 walkthrough; the verbatim verdict is `.agent/owner-W1-walkthrough-verdict-2026-10-17.md`. This is a layout correction, not a data or capability promotion. Scope is the 26 Site screens in the W1 walkthrough and W1 registry list; the registry also labels `lab` W1, but it is not one of the 26 requested screens and needs its own Lab comparison before a later owner session. The registry and its checker stay unchanged.
+
+## Binding layout contract for W1 rework and W2+ UI
+
+For every named screen, render the v69 panel **set, order, titles, relative prominence and size, grid, and visual style** from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, `screen-manifest.json`, and `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`. Use the mock's page heading, compact metric, panel, two-column, timeline, badge, table and gap patterns; port missing tokens/patterns into `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and `primitives.css` or shared UI components. Existing type/colour tokens that already match should be reused. Compare the rendered mock at both widths; the JSON titles alone do not define spatial structure. The mock's illustrative values never become product values.
+
+The panel title map below is exhaustive. Default title is exactly `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Approved plain-language changes are: `foundation:2` **Identity and operating obligations** for `Identity & operating obligations`; `dispatch:2` **Selected block · {actual selected interval}** for the mock's illustrative `14:20–14:35`; `readiness:2` **Gateway status · {actual gateway name or “No gateway selected”}** for `GW-MG-01`; `finding-evidence:1` **Evidence bundle · {actual Finding name or “Selected Finding”}** for `F-104`; `sld:1` **{actual site name} · {configured topology type, if stated}** for `Kobo Mini-grid · AC-coupled mini-grid`. Dynamic titles must use the selected read, never mock fixtures. An unknown dynamic value is labelled unknown in the same panel. No other title substitutions are approved here.
+
+Each mock panel retains its slot when a selected read cannot support its content. Put the precise honest gap **inside that panel** (unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, not assessed, absent, or proved zero as appropriate); never synthesize a value, omit the panel, or fill it with a different period's value. A page-level refusal or no-version state may state the refusal before the panel grid, but still renders the panel skeletons with in-panel gaps where the route and screen are valid. Place current app-only content within its owning mock panel or the named contextual Analysis/Evidence/History destination below. Remove the W1-UX leading answer cards and duplicate old panels after merging their supported answer, visible state/scope/as-of, and next action into the matching mock panel. No new leading card. Keep material limits visible beside the claim.
+
+`D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` and `-w1-ux-disclosure` still govern contextual Analysis tabs, narrow exact-reference disclosures, plain words and honest claims. At 390 px the same Analysis destination stays accessible. A closed disclosure cannot hold substantive method or hide a material limit. `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail` and `-w1-ux-06` continue to govern navigation and the configuration/contact/condition distinction.
+
+## Enforcement and review gate
+
+| Gate | Required proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Panel structure | Add a route-render test for **each of the 26 screen IDs**, with stocked and meaningful empty/refused variants. Read expected indexed titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` plus the five approved dynamic/plain-language rules above; query only main-content panel headings, assert exact count and DOM order, and reject any extra leading card/panel before panel 1. Include the selected tab's panels, not hidden tabs/drawers or shell headings. Assert in-panel gap text for unsupported reads. A route with a valid screen identity must not turn into a wholly different error-card layout. |
+| Visual evidence | Extend the existing W1 evidence capture tools to save **app and mock side by side** at 1440 and 390 px for every screen, with the same route state and visible panel sequence. Capture one supported and one significant missing/held state per area; log app URL, mock hash, fixture/read selection, revision, viewport, and image paths. Compare top fold, panel widths/heights, grid, density, typography, color, spacing and mobile flow. Where mock sample content differs, judge structure and truthful gap treatment. |
+| Independent review | Reviewer checks the structure test, every capture pair and mock CSS patterns. **Any panel set/order/title/grid/prominence mismatch is High**, even if copy tests pass; a leading answer card is High. Unsupported claims and hidden material limits retain their existing severity. No owner session until automated checks and side-by-side review pass for all 26 at both widths; W1 remains unaccepted until the owner accepts the reworked screens. |
+
+The title test is an exact structure guard, not a pixel test. Reviewers must judge relative sizes and visual rhythm from captures. Do not weaken a test to preserve an extra app panel; move its content to the owning slot or contextual destination.
+
+## Screen map
+
+Panel numbers and titles below use `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Each numbered clause names the current component/read fields that can fill the panel; `gap` names unsupported mock material. “Move” tells the implementer what to do with the current extra content. All rows require an in-panel read-state gap if their named read fails. The `v69-panel-contracts.json` read-model names are intent; current shipped clients, not that candidate contract, own actual claims.
+
+| ID / size | v69 panels → current component and supported read; honest gap | Move current extra content |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `sites` S | **1 Site inventory** → `SitesIndex`/`SiteTable`, `siteDirectoryClient` site name, type, configuration origin and available status. Counts, assessment/top issue: “Not available from this list” until a proved directory summary exists. **2 In setup** → current site setup/registration status only where returned; otherwise “Setup history not available.” | Fold directory answer card and lineage into inventory rows/detail; keep setup in panel 2, not a leading summary. |
+| `mini` M **large restructure** | **1 What happened in the selected hour** → `SiteWindowOverview` selected day/assessed periods and `AssessedPeriodTimeline`; say selected day rather than hour when only a day read exists, with in-panel “hour not selected” gap. **2 Operating context** → `SiteWindowOverview` context, source/coverage and objective; unsupported five-objective strip/KPI tiles show individual unknown cells. **3 Needs attention** → `OverviewFindingList`/`findingIndexClient` current rows, with pending/stale/empty distinguished. **4 Bounded money consequence** → `SiteWindowOverview` money + `financialsClient` current gated range; no current amount if held/unpriced. **5 Next step** → existing Finding/Dispatch/Action links, or “No supported next action.” | Replace `This day`, Day timeline, Findings, domain cards and W1 answer card with the five-slot grid. Preserve selected-day navigation and exact Finding bridge within owning panels. The mock is selected-hour composition; current day read cannot claim an hour-wide objective. |
+| `foundation` M **large restructure** | **1 Configured topology** → `StoredFoundationViews`/`SiteConfigurationComponents`, `foundation.detail.foundation.components`, connections and selected version. **2 Identity & operating obligations** → `SiteConfigurationFacts`, site identity, version/effective period, obligations; title changes to **Identity and operating obligations**. **3 Time-valid control configuration** → `SiteControlsTable`/`SiteControlAssumptions`, selected version controls and policy/validity, with named missing settings. | Remove “What is configured for this period?” and bulky “View configuration readiness” from the lead. Put policy in panel 3; link to the separate Readiness screen. Keep publication and exact version detail in the owning controls panel or small reference disclosure. |
+| `readiness` M | **1 Readiness and commissioning evidence** → `ReadinessCards`/`ReadinessEvidenceTable`, configured checks and bindings, “Set up to report” only for configuration. **2 Gateway status · GW-MG-01** → `GatewayStatusPanel`/`gatewayHealthClient` selected gateway contact, dynamic title; unknown if no selected gateway. **3 Connected sources** → gateway source contacts and binding coverage; gap for unstated source condition. **4 Effect on readiness and confidence** → `CapabilityReadiness`, available capability/read limits; no inference from contact alone. | Merge setup-step answer/strip into panel 1; keep gateway contact in 2 and source/analytic confidence in 3/4, without duplicating a large Foundation readiness block. |
+| `sld` M | **1 Kobo Mini-grid · AC-coupled mini-grid** → `SiteSldPrimary`/`ConfiguredSingleLineDrawing`, actual site/topology title, components and connections from selected Foundation. Unknown energized state stays unknown. **2 What each part does** → `SiteSldDetails`, selected component properties and role/binding meaning. | Put configuration status/selected part inside 1/2; remove leading answer card. Retain contained diagram scrolling at 390 only if all parts remain accessible. |
+| `evidence` M | **1 What we can conclude now** → `SiteEvidence`/`CapabilityReadinessTable`, accepted record coverage and current analytic limit; if no assessment, “No conclusion yet.” **2 Data sources · data quality, not equipment condition** → `SiteEvidence` source status/`ledgerEvidenceClient`, receipt, source time and rejection distinctions. **3 Most useful next evidence** → missing required roles/coverage from capability read; otherwise “Next evidence not identified.” | Merge W1 summary into 1; source/coverage/rejected-record detail in 2 or contextual Evidence/Analysis, not extra top panels. |
+| `evidence-analysis` S | **1 Accepted records behind the conclusions** → `EvidenceClaimLimit`, assessed-period accepted record/role spans, exact links. **2 How a claim limit is set** → `EvidenceClaimLimit` reason, role capability and limit explanation; no selected assessment = no limit claim. | Put record inspector and long lineage below within these panels/contextual detail; no repeated answer card. |
+| `dispatch` M | **1 Sources and 15-minute blocks** → `PerformanceDispatch`, selected-day period/block strip, accepted input status. **2 Selected block · 14:20–14:35** → actual selected interval/assessment result and reported values; dynamic title. **3 Accepted sample coverage** → `PerformanceDispatch` coverage/readiness and source links; missing roles named. | Merge W1 answer and duplicate block chooser into 1/2; move comparison/history/technical record to Analysis or existing history context. |
+| `analysis` S | **1 Constraint comparison** → `PerformanceDispatch` Analysis constraint rows and interval inputs. **2 Bounded fuel calculation** → `FinancialConsequence`/current consequence when eligible; otherwise “No supported calculation.” **3 Coverage, confidence and alternatives** → `PerformanceDispatch` limit/alternatives and accepted coverage; unknown alternatives stay unknown. | Move analysis story, gated fuel panel and record detail into these three, retaining exact references in small disclosure or Evidence. |
+| `sitefindings` S | **1 Findings at this site** → `SiteFindingIndex`/`findingIndexClient`, current/pending/stale rows, selected scope and known count. Unsupported priority/confidence chips are honest unknown. | Merge answer card, basis and filters into the single panel header/table; no second lead. |
+| `finding` M | **1 What happened** → `SiteFinding` selected `FindingRevision` claim, span and currentness. **2 What limits this conclusion** → undecided portions, cited assessment/coverage and source limits. **3 What to do next** → supported Recommendation/Action link when present; otherwise “No recorded next action.” **4 Where to look next** → Evidence, History and Analysis links with exact selection. | Move versions, record lists and raw relation panels to History/Evidence/Analysis; merge W1 answer into 1 and state/limits into 1/2. |
+| `finding-evidence` S | **1 Evidence bundle · F-104** → `SiteFindingEvidence` cited assessments and accepted record bundle, dynamic actual Finding title. **2 Inspect source lineage** → `SiteFindingEvidence` record trail and unresolved citations; say which links fail. | Fold “What this version cites”, assessments and cited-record panels into 1; selected record inspector in 2/contextual detail. |
+| `finding-history` S | **1 Revision history** → `SiteFindingHistory` revision list, recorded times, lifecycle and relation changes; one version = “No earlier versions.” | Move related Findings into each revision's context or a link to the index; merge W1 history summary into the panel. |
+| `financials` M | **1 What happened → what it means in money** → `SiteFinancialsOverview`/`FindingBridge`, current consequence revision, bounded fuel range only through money gates. **2 Assumptions in use** → selected diesel price/version and basis; missing price stays unpriced. **3 Trade-offs and exclusions** → consequence limits/source gate; revenue, asset value and realised savings = “Not established” separately. | Replace leading amount card, bridge steps and price panel hierarchy with three mock panels; history lives on Assumptions/Interpretation history. |
+| `assumptions` S | **1 Financial assumptions** → `SiteAssumptions`, exact price revision, currency, unit and effective/recorded periods; no price = “No price published.” **2 Impact preview** → current consequence comparison only if an authorized preview read exists; otherwise “Preview not available” and no invented slider result. **3 Interpretation history** → `ConsequenceHistoryPanel` and price history, labelled historical at cutoff. | Put existing price, assignment and history controls into 1/3; disabled edit stays inside 1 with reason. |
+| `recommendation` M | **1 Proposed intervention** → `SiteRecommendation` live/accepted proposal, limits and prior-action hold. **2 Declare success before evaluating** → accepted verification-plan criteria when present; otherwise “No success criteria recorded.” **3 Evidence needed to verify** → plan evidence requirements; otherwise “Requirements not recorded.” | Merge W1 answer, battery trial, accepted proposal and live recomputation into 1; acceptance control there. Put money as a qualified link to Financials, not another leading card. |
+| `changes` S | **1 Change and effect** → `SiteFoundationChange`, policy change, before/after selected Foundation revisions, application outcome; no post-change effect = pending. **2 Decision path** → linked Action, plan and verification outcome; no plan/outcome = “Not recorded yet.” | Merge “What changed”, Outcome, Application and version panels into 1; put decision references in 2. |
+| `action-overview` M | **1 Action scope** → `SiteAction`, origin Finding, accepted trial, status and foundation change from `actionClient`; verification remains pending unless result exists. | Merge W1 answer, Origin, Latest Finding, Accepted trial and Application into this single mock panel; direct detailed activity to Execution and result to Verification. |
+| `action-plan` S | **1 Success criteria** → `SiteActionVerification` plan target, guardrails and selected windows; no plan = “No success criteria recorded.” | Merge W1 answer and separate Windows into one panel; exact selection remains in URL. |
+| `action-execution` S | **1 Activity log** → `SiteAction` ActionEvents accepted/applied/failed/observed events, actor/time and application state; no events = “No activity recorded.” | Merge Recorded activity and apply controls into the log context; remove duplicate top answer. |
+| `action-evidence` S | **1 Evidence requirements** → `SiteActionVerification` plan bindings, target/guardrail source requirements and observed availability; missing plan/source named. | Merge requirements and per-window evidence into one panel; deeper source inspection links to Evidence. |
+| `action-history` S | **1 Action history** → `SiteAction` event selector/history and `verificationClient` outcome history, with earlier/current labels. | Merge Recorded events and W1 card; keep exact event/cutoff selection in the panel. |
+| `verification` M | **1 Target and guardrails evaluated together** → `ActionVerification` plan/outcome metrics, currentness and guardrail failure/inconclusive reasons; no outcome = pending. **2 Activity evidence** → accepted/applied Action events; activity alone never proves result. **3 Outcome evidence** → baseline/trial source windows, comparability and analytic outcome; missing comparison explicitly inconclusive. | Merge W1 answer, Outcome, Target/limits and guardrail panels into 1. Existing plan/windows/requirements link to Action Plan/Evidence; no duplicate result panel. |
+| `sitework` S | **1 Actions at this site** → `SiteWorkActions`/`actionIndexClient` current rows, scope, read time, accepted/applied/result states; proved empty distinct from unavailable. | Merge W1 answer, filters and list into one panel; no separate top card. |
+| `health` M | **1 Physical asset condition** → `SiteHealth`/`healthClient` condition boundary and supported battery summary; where no physical condition read exists, prominently “Condition cannot yet be judged.” **2 Data sources · data quality, not equipment condition** → gateway/source contact and fact age from `gatewayHealthClient`/`healthClient`; stale/unknown contact distinct. | Put gateway/contact cards in 2 and condition in 1; remove leading answer and any implication that contact proves condition. |
+| `asset` M | **1 Observed reserve and power exposure** → `SiteBattery` selected battery, observed SOC/power windows and source qualification. **2 Interpretation and next evidence** → bounded reserve interpretation and capacity-test need; no capacity test = gap. **3 Condition evidence and claim boundary** → `SiteBattery`/`healthClient` condition boundary; no RUL or physical health claim. | Merge battery answer and configured-battery selector into 1; move general gateway status to Health, not another battery condition card. |

+

+| --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| F1 shared guard + Foundation | M | `sites`, `foundation`, `readiness`, `sld` | Establish exact panel test, title map, shared CSS patterns, capture pairs. Directory assessment/count and Site setup-event history lack a proved read; keep panel gaps. |

+| F3 Findings + money | M | `sitefindings`, `finding`, `finding-evidence`, `finding-history`, `financials`, `assumptions` | Keep current gated money and exact-history reads. Editable assumption/impact-preview read and revenue/asset/realised-savings measures are genuinely missing. |
+| F4 Action + work | M | `recommendation`, `changes`, `action-overview`, `action-plan`, `action-execution`, `action-evidence`, `action-history`, `verification`, `sitework` | Rehome duplicate Action cards and retain exact selectors/guardrails. +| `foundation` M **large restructure** | **1 Configured topology** → `StoredFoundationViews`/`SiteConfigurationComponents`, `foundation.detail.foundation.components`, connections and selected version. **2 Identity & operating obligations** → `SiteConfigurationFacts`, site identity, version/effective period, obligations; title changes to **Identity and operating obligations**. **3 Time-valid control configuration** → `SiteControlsTable`/`SiteControlAssumptions`, selected version controls and policy/validity, with named missing settings. | Remove “What is configured for this period?” and bulky “View configuration readiness” from the lead. Put policy in panel 3; link to the separate Readiness screen. Keep publication and exact version detail in the owning controls panel or small reference disclosure. |
+| `readiness` M | **1 Readiness and commissioning evidence** → `ReadinessCards`/`ReadinessEvidenceTable`, configured checks and bindings, “Set up to report” only for configuration. **2 Gateway status · GW-MG-01** → `GatewayStatusPanel`/`gatewayHealthClient` selected gateway contact, dynamic title; unknown if no selected gateway. **3 Connected sources** → gateway source contacts and binding coverage; gap for unstated source condition. **4 Effect on readiness and confidence** → `CapabilityReadiness`, available capability/read limits; no inference from contact alone. | Merge setup-step answer/strip into panel 1; keep gateway contact in 2 and source/analytic confidence in 3/4, without duplicating a large Foundation readiness block. |
+| `sld` M | **1 Kobo Mini-grid · AC-coupled mini-grid** → `SiteSldPrimary`/`ConfiguredSingleLineDrawing`, actual site/topology title, components and connections from selected Foundation. Unknown energized state stays unknown. **2 What each part does** → `SiteSldDetails`, selected component properties and role/binding meaning. | Put configuration status/selected part inside 1/2; remove leading answer card. Retain contained diagram scrolling at 390 only if all parts remain accessible. |
+| `evidence` M | **1 What we can conclude now** → `SiteEvidence`/`CapabilityReadinessTable`, accepted record coverage and current analytic limit; if no assessment, “No conclusion yet.” **2 Data sources · data quality, not equipment condition** → `SiteEvidence` source status/`ledgerEvidenceClient`, receipt, source time and rejection distinctions. **3 Most useful next evidence** → missing required roles/coverage from capability read; otherwise “Next evidence not identified.” | Merge W1 summary into 1; source/coverage/rejected-record detail in 2 or contextual Evidence/Analysis, not extra top panels. |
+| `evidence-analysis` S | **1 Accepted records behind the conclusions** → `EvidenceClaimLimit`, assessed-period accepted record/role spans, exact links. **2 How a claim limit is set** → `EvidenceClaimLimit` reason, role capability and limit explanation; no selected assessment = no limit claim. | Put record inspector and long lineage below within these panels/contextual detail; no repeated answer card. |
+| `dispatch` M | **1 Sources and 15-minute blocks** → `PerformanceDispatch`, selected-day period/block strip, accepted input status. **2 Selected block · 14:20–14:35** → actual selected interval/assessment result and reported values; dynamic title. **3 Accepted sample coverage** → `PerformanceDispatch` coverage/readiness and source links; missing roles named. | Merge W1 answer and duplicate block chooser into 1/2; move comparison/history/technical record to Analysis or existing history context. |
+| `analysis` S | **1 Constraint comparison** → `PerformanceDispatch` Analysis constraint rows and interval inputs. **2 Bounded fuel calculation** → `FinancialConsequence`/current consequence when eligible; otherwise “No supported calculation.” **3 Coverage, confidence and alternatives** → `PerformanceDispatch` limit/alternatives and accepted coverage; unknown alternatives stay unknown. | Move analysis story, gated fuel panel and record detail into these three, retaining exact references in small disclosure or Evidence. |
+| `sitefindings` S | **1 Findings at this site** → `SiteFindingIndex`/`findingIndexClient`, current/pending/stale rows, selected scope and known count. Unsupported priority/confidence chips are honest unknown. | Merge answer card, basis and filters into the single panel header/table; no second lead. |
+| `finding` M | **1 What happened** → `SiteFinding` selected `FindingRevision` claim, span and currentness. **2 What limits this conclusion** → undecided portions, cited assessment/coverage and source limits. **3 What to do next** → supported Recommendation/Action link when present; otherwise “No recorded next action.” **4 Where to look next** → Evidence, History and Analysis links with exact selection. | Move versions, record lists and raw relation panels to History/Evidence/Analysis; merge W1 answer into 1 and state/limits into 1/2. |
+| `finding-evidence` S | **1 Evidence bundle · F-104** → `SiteFindingEvidence` cited assessments and accepted record bundle, dynamic actual Finding title. **2 Inspect source lineage** → `SiteFindingEvidence` record trail and unresolved citations; say which links fail. | Fold “What this version cites”, assessments and cited-record panels into 1; selected record inspector in 2/contextual detail. |
+| `finding-history` S | **1 Revision history** → `SiteFindingHistory` revision list, recorded times, lifecycle and relation changes; one version = “No earlier versions.” | Move related Findings into each revision's context or a link to the index; merge W1 history summary into the panel. |
+| `financials` M | **1 What happened → what it means in money** → `SiteFinancialsOverview`/`FindingBridge`, current consequence revision, bounded fuel range only through money gates. **2 Assumptions in use** → selected diesel price/version and basis; missing price stays unpriced. **3 Trade-offs and exclusions** → consequence limits/source gate; revenue, asset value and realised savings = “Not established” separately. | Replace leading amount card, bridge steps and price panel hierarchy with three mock panels; history lives on Assumptions/Interpretation history. |

Existing plan/windows/requirements link to Action Plan/Evidence; no duplicate result panel. |
+| `sitework` S | **1 Actions at this site** → `SiteWorkActions`/`actionIndexClient` current rows, scope, read time, accepted/applied/result states; proved empty distinct from unavailable. | Merge W1 answer, filters and list into one panel; no separate top card. |
+| `health` M | **1 Physical asset condition** → `SiteHealth`/`healthClient` condition boundary and supported battery summary; where no physical condition read exists, prominently “Condition cannot yet be judged.” **2 Data sources · data quality, not equipment condition** → gateway/source contact and fact age from `gatewayHealthClient`/`healthClient`; stale/unknown contact distinct. | Put gateway/contact cards in 2 and condition in 1; remove leading answer and any implication that contact proves condition. |
+| `asset` M | **1 Observed reserve and power exposure** → `SiteBattery` selected battery, observed SOC/power windows and source qualification. **2 Interpretation and next evidence** → bounded reserve interpretation and capacity-test need; no capacity test = gap. **3 Condition evidence and claim boundary** → `SiteBattery`/`healthClient` condition boundary; no RUL or physical health claim. | Merge battery answer and configured-battery selector into 1; move general gateway status to Health, not another battery condition card. |

+# W1 structural fidelity: Architect contract and rework plan (2026-10-17)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. The owner rejected the combined W1 walkthrough; the verbatim verdict is `.agent/owner-W1-walkthrough-verdict-2026-10-17.md`. This is a layout correction, not a data or capability promotion. Scope is the 26 Site screens in the W1 walkthrough and W1 registry list; the registry also labels `lab` W1, but it is not one of the 26 requested screens and needs its own Lab comparison before a later owner session. The registry and its checker stay unchanged.
+
+## Binding layout contract for W1 rework and W2+ UI
+
+For every named screen, render the v69 panel **set, order, titles, relative prominence and size, grid, and visual style** from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, `screen-manifest.json`, and `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`. Use the mock's page heading, compact metric, panel, two-column, timeline, badge, table and gap patterns; port missing tokens/patterns into `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and `primitives.css` or shared UI components. Existing type/colour tokens that already match should be reused. Compare the rendered mock at both widths; the JSON titles alone do not define spatial structure. The mock's illustrative values never become product values.
+
+The panel title map below is exhaustive. Default title is exactly `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Approved plain-language changes are: `foundation:2` **Identity and operating obligations** for `Identity & operating obligations`; `dispatch:2` **Selected block · {actual selected interval}** for the mock's illustrative `14:20–14:35`; `readiness:2` **Gateway status · {actual gateway name or “No gateway selected”}** for `GW-MG-01`; `finding-evidence:1` **Evidence bundle · {actual Finding name or “Selected Finding”}** for `F-104`; `sld:1` **{actual site name} · {configured topology type, if stated}** for `Kobo Mini-grid · AC-coupled mini-grid`. Dynamic titles must use the selected read, never mock fixtures. An unknown dynamic value is labelled unknown in the same panel. No other title substitutions are approved here.
+
+Each mock panel retains its slot when a selected read cannot support its content. Put the precise honest gap **inside that panel** (unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, not assessed, absent, or proved zero as appropriate); never synthesize a value, omit the panel, or fill it with a different period's value. A page-level refusal or no-version state may state the refusal before the panel grid, but still renders the panel skeletons with in-panel gaps where the route and screen are valid. Place current app-only content within its owning mock panel or the named contextual Analysis/Evidence/History destination below. Remove the W1-UX leading answer cards and duplicate old panels after merging their supported answer, visible state/scope/as-of, and next action into the matching mock panel. No new leading card. Keep material limits visible beside the claim.
+
+`D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` and `-w1-ux-disclosure` still govern contextual Analysis tabs, narrow exact-reference disclosures, plain words and honest claims. At 390 px the same Analysis destination stays accessible. A closed disclosure cannot hold substantive method or hide a material limit. `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail` and `-w1-ux-06` continue to govern navigation and the configuration/contact/condition distinction.
+
+## Enforcement and review gate
+
+| Gate | Required proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Panel structure | Add a route-render test for **each of the 26 screen IDs**, with stocked and meaningful empty/refused variants. Read expected indexed titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` plus the five approved dynamic/plain-language rules above; query only main-content panel headings, assert exact count and DOM order, and reject any extra leading card/panel before panel 1. Include the selected tab's panels, not hidden tabs/drawers or shell headings. Assert in-panel gap text for unsupported reads. A route with a valid screen identity must not turn into a wholly different error-card layout. |
+| Visual evidence | Extend the existing W1 evidence capture tools to save **app and mock side by side** at 1440 and 390 px for every screen, with the same route state and visible panel sequence. Capture one supported and one significant missing/held state per area; log app URL, mock hash, fixture/read selection, revision, viewport, and image paths. Compare top fold, panel widths/heights, grid, density, typography, color, spacing and mobile flow. Where mock sample content differs, judge structure and truthful gap treatment. |
+| Independent review | Reviewer checks the structure test, every capture pair and mock CSS patterns. **Any panel set/order/title/grid/prominence mismatch is High**, even if copy tests pass; a leading answer card is High. Unsupported claims and hidden material limits retain their existing severity. No owner session until automated checks and side-by-side review pass for all 26 at both widths; W1 remains unaccepted until the owner accepts the reworked screens. |
+
+The title test is an exact structure guard, not a pixel test. Reviewers must judge relative sizes and visual rhythm from captures. Do not weaken a test to preserve an extra app panel; move its content to the owning slot or contextual destination.
+

+| F5 Health + closeout | S | `health`, `asset`; all 26 capture audit | Separate source contact from condition, finish 1440/390 pairs and independent review. Physical battery condition/capacity test and RUL read are missing and remain labelled gaps. |

The missing reads above are separate product/backend work; this rework must not fill them with simulated truth or mock values.

+# W1 structural fidelity: Architect contract and rework plan (2026-10-17)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. The owner rejected the combined W1 walkthrough; the verbatim verdict is `.agent/owner-W1-walkthrough-verdict-2026-10-17.md`. This is a layout correction, not a data or capability promotion. Scope is the 26 Site screens in the W1 walkthrough and W1 registry list; the registry also labels `lab` W1, but it is not one of the 26 requested screens and needs its own Lab comparison before a later owner session. The registry and its checker stay unchanged.
+
+## Binding layout contract for W1 rework and W2+ UI
+
+For every named screen, render the v69 panel **set, order, titles, relative prominence and size, grid, and visual style** from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, `screen-manifest.json`, and `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`. Use the mock's page heading, compact metric, panel, two-column, timeline, badge, table and gap patterns; port missing tokens/patterns into `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and `primitives.css` or shared UI components. Existing type/colour tokens that already match should be reused. Compare the rendered mock at both widths; the JSON titles alone do not define spatial structure. The mock's illustrative values never become product values.
+
+The panel title map below is exhaustive. Default title is exactly `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Approved plain-language changes are: `foundation:2` **Identity and operating obligations** for `Identity & operating obligations`; `dispatch:2` **Selected block · {actual selected interval}** for the mock's illustrative `14:20–14:35`; `readiness:2` **Gateway status · {actual gateway name or “No gateway selected”}** for `GW-MG-01`; `finding-evidence:1` **Evidence bundle · {actual Finding name or “Selected Finding”}** for `F-104`; `sld:1` **{actual site name} · {configured topology type, if stated}** for `Kobo Mini-grid · AC-coupled mini-grid`. Dynamic titles must use the selected read, never mock fixtures. An unknown dynamic value is labelled unknown in the same panel. No other title substitutions are approved here.
+
+Each mock panel retains its slot when a selected read cannot support its content. Put the precise honest gap **inside that panel** (unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, not assessed, absent, or proved zero as appropriate); never synthesize a value, omit the panel, or fill it with a different period's value. A page-level refusal or no-version state may state the refusal before the panel grid, but still renders the panel skeletons with in-panel gaps where the route and screen are valid. Place current app-only content within its owning mock panel or the named contextual Analysis/Evidence/History destination below. Remove the W1-UX leading answer cards and duplicate old panels after merging their supported answer, visible state/scope/as-of, and next action into the matching mock panel. No new leading card. Keep material limits visible beside the claim.
+
+`D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` and `-w1-ux-disclosure` still govern contextual Analysis tabs, narrow exact-reference disclosures, plain words and honest claims. At 390 px the same Analysis destination stays accessible. A closed disclosure cannot hold substantive method or hide a material limit. `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail` and `-w1-ux-06` continue to govern navigation and the configuration/contact/condition distinction.
+
+## Enforcement and review gate
+
+| Gate | Required proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Panel structure | Add a route-render test for **each of the 26 screen IDs**, with stocked and meaningful empty/refused variants. Read expected indexed titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` plus the five approved dynamic/plain-language rules above; query only main-content panel headings, assert exact count and DOM order, and reject any extra leading card/panel before panel 1. Include the selected tab's panels, not hidden tabs/drawers or shell headings. Assert in-panel gap text for unsupported reads. A route with a valid screen identity must not turn into a wholly different error-card layout. |
+| Visual evidence | Extend the existing W1 evidence capture tools to save **app and mock side by side** at 1440 and 390 px for every screen, with the same route state and visible panel sequence. Capture one supported and one significant missing/held state per area; log app URL, mock hash, fixture/read selection, revision, viewport, and image paths. Compare top fold, panel widths/heights, grid, density, typography, color, spacing and mobile flow. Where mock sample content differs, judge structure and truthful gap treatment. |
+| Independent review | Reviewer checks the structure test, every capture pair and mock CSS patterns. **Any panel set/order/title/grid/prominence mismatch is High**, even if copy tests pass; a leading answer card is High. Unsupported claims and hidden material limits retain their existing severity. No owner session until automated checks and side-by-side review pass for all 26 at both widths; W1 remains unaccepted until the owner accepts the reworked screens. |
+
+The title test is an exact structure guard, not a pixel test. Reviewers must judge relative sizes and visual rhythm from captures. Do not weaken a test to preserve an extra app panel; move its content to the owning slot or contextual destination.
+
+## Screen map
+
+Panel numbers and titles below use `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Each numbered clause names the current component/read fields that can fill the panel; `gap` names unsupported mock material. “Move” tells the implementer what to do with the current extra content. All rows require an in-panel read-state gap if their named read fails. The `v69-panel-contracts.json` read-model names are intent; current shipped clients, not that candidate contract, own actual claims.
+
+| ID / size | v69 panels → current component and supported read; honest gap | Move current extra content |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `sites` S | **1 Site inventory** → `SitesIndex`/`SiteTable`, `siteDirectoryClient` site name, type, configuration origin and available status. Counts, assessment/top issue: “Not available from this list” until a proved directory summary exists. **2 In setup** → current site setup/registration status only where returned; otherwise “Setup history not available.” | Fold directory answer card and lineage into inventory rows/detail; keep setup in panel 2, not a leading summary. |
+| `mini` M **large restructure** | **1 What happened in the selected hour** → `SiteWindowOverview` selected day/assessed periods and `AssessedPeriodTimeline`; say selected day rather than hour when only a day read exists, with in-panel “hour not selected” gap. **2 Operating context** → `SiteWindowOverview` context, source/coverage and objective; unsupported five-objective strip/KPI tiles show individual unknown cells. **3 Needs attention** → `OverviewFindingList`/`findingIndexClient` current rows, with pending/stale/empty distinguished. **4 Bounded money consequence** → `SiteWindowOverview` money + `financialsClient` current gated range; no current amount if held/unpriced. **5 Next step** → existing Finding/Dispatch/Action links, or “No supported next action.” | Replace `This day`, Day timeline, Findings, domain cards and W1 answer card with the five-slot grid. Preserve selected-day navigation and exact Finding bridge within owning panels. The mock is selected-hour composition; current day read cannot claim an hour-wide objective. |
+| `foundation` M **large restructure** | **1 Configured topology** → `StoredFoundationViews`/`SiteConfigurationComponents`, `foundation.detail.foundation.components`, connections and selected version. **2 Identity & operating obligations** → `SiteConfigurationFacts`, site identity, version/effective period, obligations; title changes to **Identity and operating obligations**. **3 Time-valid control configuration** → `SiteControlsTable`/`SiteControlAssumptions`, selected version controls and policy/validity, with named missing settings. | Remove “What is configured for this period?” and bulky “View configuration readiness” from the lead. Put policy in panel 3; link to the separate Readiness screen. Keep publication and exact version detail in the owning controls panel or small reference disclosure. |
+| `readiness` M | **1 Readiness and commissioning evidence** → `ReadinessCards`/`ReadinessEvidenceTable`, configured checks and bindings, “Set up to report” only for configuration. **2 Gateway status · GW-MG-01** → `GatewayStatusPanel`/`gatewayHealthClient` selected gateway contact, dynamic title; unknown if no selected gateway. **3 Connected sources** → gateway source contacts and binding coverage; gap for unstated source condition. **4 Effect on readiness and confidence** → `CapabilityReadiness`, available capability/read limits; no inference from contact alone. | Merge setup-step answer/strip into panel 1; keep gateway contact in 2 and source/analytic confidence in 3/4, without duplicating a large Foundation readiness block. |
